Buff/Ability Types ^VOI0tY5d

Link buff:
- Depending on the pattern, gain buff/ability

Linked Type count buff:
- If linked pattern has x number of y type, gain buff
- Examples:
    - 3 archers
    - 2 archers + 1 magic
    - 1 archer + 1 magic + 1 troop

Tower link abilities:
- Only linking specific towers activates the ability
- Special abilities!

Card system
- Increase store odds
- Increase stats for types
    - Attack Speed, 
    - Ability Cooldown
- Increase EXP/Gold/Loot
